Accordion

views-accordion · ./src/components/views/Accordion/Accordion.stories.tsx
Accordion component - collapsible sections with optional expand/collapse all control.
Prop types (react-docgen) 11 prop types 
Component: src/components/views/Accordion/Accordion.tsx::Accordion
Props:
/**
 * Whether to allow toggling individual items
 */
allowToggle?: boolean = true

/**
 * Children (slot content for items)
 */
children?: ReactNode

/**
 * CSS classes for the accordion container
 */
classes?: string

/**
 * Heading configuration
 */
heading?: AccordionHeadingData

/**
 * Heading level (1-5) for the accordion heading
 */
headingLevel?: number = 2

/**
 * Unique identifier prefix for the accordion
 */
idPrefix?: string

/**
 * Array of accordion items
 */
items?: AccordionItemData[] = []

/**
 * Called when expand/collapse all is clicked
 */
onChangeAll?: (showAll: boolean) => void

/**
 * Called when an item's open state changes
 */
onToggleItem?: (item: AccordionItemData, index: number) => void

/**
 * Whether all items should be open initially
 */
showAll?: boolean = false

/**
 * Whether to show a control to expand/collapse all
 */
showControl?: boolean = false
Imports

Modal

modals-modal · ./src/components/modals/Modal/Modal.stories.tsx
Modal component - displays a dialog with title, description, optional icon, and action buttons.
Prop types (react-docgen) 16 prop types 
Component: src/components/modals/Modal/Modal.tsx::Modal
Props:
/**
 * Child content
 */
children?: ReactNode

/**
 * Custom CSS classes for the modal
 */
className?: string

/**
 * Description text
 */
description?: string

/**
 * Description CSS classes
 */
descriptionClasses?: string

/**
 * Description HTML content
 */
descriptionHtml?: string

/**
 * Heading level for title (1-5)
 */
headingLevel?: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 = 2

/**
 * Icon type: 'discard', 'delete', 'publish', 'changes', 'edit'
 */
icon?: 'discard' | 'delete' | 'publish' | 'changes' | 'edit'

/**
 * Unique identifier
 */
id: string

/**
 * Whether the modal is dismissible
 */
isDismissible?: boolean

/**
 * Primary buttons
 */
itemsPrimary?: ModalButton[]

/**
 * Secondary buttons
 */
itemsSecondary?: ModalButton[]

/**
 * Button click handler
 */
onButtonClick?: (button: ModalButton, event: React.MouseEvent) => void

/**
 * Close modal handler
 */
onClose?: () => void

/**
 * Modal title text
 */
title?: string

/**
 * Modal title CSS classes
 */
titleClasses?: string

/**
 * Modal title HTML content
 */
titleHtml?: string

 * Render as switch (checkbox-like) or regular toggle

 * Change event handler

 */

Tree

forms-tree · ./src/components/forms/Tree/Tree.stories.tsx
Tree component - hierarchical list with checkboxes or radio buttons. Supports single/multiple selection, nested items, and keyboard navigation.
Prop types (react-docgen) 12 prop types 
Component: src/components/forms/Tree/Tree.tsx::Tree
Props:
/**
 * Children slot
 */
children?: ReactNode

/**
 * CSS classes for the tree
 */
classes?: string

/**
 * Decouple child selection from parent
 */

/**
 * Whether to expand first level by default
 */
expandedFirstLevel?: boolean

/**
 * Whether to show dividers
 */
hasDividers?: boolean

/**
 * Unique identifier
 */
id?: string

/**
 * Array of tree items
 */

/**
 * Item name prefix for form submission
 */

/**
 * Called when selection changes
 */
onChange?: (item: TreeItemData) => void

/**
 * Called when item is expanded/collapsed
 */
onExpand?: (item: TreeItemData, expanded: boolean) => void

/**
 * Search/filter text
 */

/**
 * Tree type: radio (single select) or checkbox (multi select)
 */

import { Tree } from "desy-react";
Arbol Simple story ok 
const ArbolSimple = () => {
    const [items, setItems] = useState<TreeItemData[]>(args.items || []);

    return (
        <Tree
            id="tree-checkbox-1"
            name="tree-options-1"
            type="checkbox"
            expandedFirstLevel
            hasDividers
            items={items}
            onChange={(changedItem) => {
              setItems((prev) => updateNestedItem(prev, changedItem.id || '', { checked: changedItem.checked }));
            }} />
    );
};

Arbol Simple Con Todos Los Elementos Inicial Expandidos story ok 
const ArbolSimpleConTodosLosElementosInicialExpandidos = () => {
    const [items, setItems] = useState<TreeItemData[]>(
      (args.items || []).map((item: TreeItemData) => ({
        ...item,
        expanded: true,
        items: item.items?.map((c: TreeItemData) => ({ ...c, expanded: true })),
      }))
    );

    return (
        <Tree
            id="tree-checkbox-3"
            name="tree-options-3"
            type="checkbox"
            hasDividers
            items={items}
            onChange={(changedItem) => {
              setItems((prev) => updateNestedItem(prev, changedItem.id || '', { checked: changedItem.checked }));
            }} />
    );
};
